SELECT a.course, a.college, e.title AS job_title, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM alumni a JOIN alumni_experience e ON a.alumni_id = e.alumni_id AND e.current = 1 WHERE e.employment_status IS NOT NULL AND e.employment_status <> '' AND e.title IS NOT NULL AND e.title <> '' AND a.course IS NOT NULL AND a.course <> '' AND a.year_graduated = 2020 GROUP BY a.course, a.college, e.title
```
